    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_map_opened NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase has_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase } check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 3 } GER_region_action_13_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 13 } }   # 红色海军网络
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_map_opened NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase has_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase } check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 14 } GER_region_action_14_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 14 } }   # 与基督教社会主义者交好
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_15_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 15 } }   # 调停SPD内部冲突
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_16_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 16 } }   # 对巴伐利亚施压
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_17_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 17 } }   # 向协约国提交巴伐利亚报告
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_18_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 18 } }   # 采取保守态势
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_19_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 19 } }   # 宣布抵制恐怖袭击
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_20_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 20 } }   # 撤出SPD优秀分子
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_21_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 21 } }   # 向巴伐利亚发动最后通牒
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 } GER_region_action_22_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 22 } }   # 依靠协约国施压
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 7 } GER_region_action_23_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 23 } }   # 派遣正规军
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 7 } GER_region_action_24_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 24 } }   # 向协约国控诉
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase check_variable = { GER_region_selected = 7 } GER_region_action_25_visible = yes } add_to_array = { array = GER_region_panel_list value = 25 } }   # 加强对激进派的控制
}

# ---- 给手写决议效果用的小工具。先 set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = 3 }（或 GER_region_value），再调用。
# 支持度、控制度结果夹在 0-100；控制度不低于该地区的下限（影子苏维埃后为 80）。
# 指定地区：GER_region_Rxx_add_support / _add_control（Rxx 见区块 key）
# 西里西亚：支持度、控制度不会增长（GER_region_delta > 0 时不加），国策 / 焦点博弈先设 GER_region_force = 1 再调用
GER_region_R01_add_support = {   # 威悉-埃姆斯
    add_to_variable = { GER_region_R01_support = GER_region_delta }
    clamp_variable = { var = GER_region_R01_support min = 0 max = 100 }
}
GER_region_R01_add_control = {   # 威悉-埃姆斯
    add_to_variable = { GER_region_R01_control = GER_region_delta }
    clamp_variable = { var = GER_region_R01_control min = 0 max = 100 }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control < GER_region_R01_control_floor } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_control = GER_region_R01_control_floor } }
}

    add_to_variable = { GER_region_R02_support = GER_region_delta }
    clamp_variable = { var = GER_region_R02_support min = 0 max = 100 }
}

# 作用于 GER_region_target 指向的地区（决议效果执行时已经设好）
GER_region_target_add_support = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 } } GER_region_R01_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 } } GER_region_R02_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 3 } } GER_region_R03_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 4 } } GER_region_R04_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 5 } } GER_region_R05_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 6 } } GER_region_R06_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 7 } } GER_region_R07_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 8 } } GER_region_R08_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 9 } } GER_region_R09_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 10 } } GER_region_R10_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 11 } } GER_region_R11_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 12 } } GER_region_R12_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 13 } } GER_region_R13_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 14 } } GER_region_R14_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 15 } } GER_region_R15_add_support = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 16 } } GER_region_R16_add_support = yes }
}
GER_region_target_add_control = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 } } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 3 } } GER_region_R03_add_control = yes }

# 目标地区的相邻地区控制度 + GER_region_delta（相邻关系见 gen_region_game.py 的 ADJ）
GER_region_target_neighbors_add_control = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R15_add_control = yes }   # 威悉-埃姆斯
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 } } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes GER_region_R03_add_control = yes GER_region_R04_add_control = yes GER_region_R06_add_control = yes GER_region_R08_add_control = yes GER_region_R11_add_control = yes GER_region_R15_add_control = yes }   # 汉诺威
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 3 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R04_add_control = yes }   # 汉堡-石荷
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 4 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R03_add_control = yes GER_region_R05_add_control = yes GER_region_R06_add_control = yes }   # 梅克伦堡
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 5 } } GER_region_R04_add_control = yes GER_region_R06_add_control = yes GER_region_R13_add_control = yes }   # 波美拉尼亚
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 6 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R04_add_control = yes GER_region_R05_add_control = yes GER_region_R07_add_control = yes GER_region_R08_add_control = yes GER_region_R13_add_control = yes GER_region_R16_add_control = yes }   # 勃兰登堡
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 7 } } GER_region_R06_add_control = yes GER_region_R08_add_control = yes }   # 西里西亚
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 8 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R06_add_control = yes GER_region_R07_add_control = yes GER_region_R09_add_control = yes GER_region_R11_add_control = yes }   # 图林根-萨克森
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 9 } } GER_region_R08_add_control = yes GER_region_R10_add_control = yes GER_region_R11_add_control = yes }   # 巴伐利亚
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 10 } } GER_region_R09_add_control = yes GER_region_R11_add_control = yes GER_region_R12_add_control = yes }   # 巴登-符腾堡
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 11 } } GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R08_add_control = yes GER_region_R09_add_control = yes GER_region_R10_add_control = yes GER_region_R12_add_control = yes GER_region_R15_add_control = yes }   # 黑森
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 12 } } GER_region_R10_add_control = yes GER_region_R11_add_control = yes GER_region_R14_add_control = yes GER_region_R15_add_control = yes }   # 莱茵兰
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 13 } } GER_region_R05_add_control = yes GER_region_R06_add_control = yes }   # 东普鲁士
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 14 } } GER_region_R12_add_control = yes GER_region_R15_add_control = yes }   # 鲁尔
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 15 } } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes GER_region_R02_add_control = yes GER_region_R11_add_control = yes GER_region_R12_add_control = yes GER_region_R14_add_control = yes }   # 威斯特法伦
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 16 } } GER_region_R06_add_control = yes }   # 大柏林
}
# 目标地区类型 = GER_region_value（帧号：2 摇摆 3 左核 8 左控 ……）
GER_region_target_set_state = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_state = GER_region_value } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R02_state = GER_region_value } }

    GER_region_action_22 = yes
    GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
}

# ---- 每天（on_daily_GER）：日常决议倒计时，每满 14 天调用 _biweekly，到 0 调用 _complete；
# 驻区方针累计天数，每满 14 天调用当前方针的 _biweekly。
# 另外：驻区方针每天调用 _daily；全国每满 14 天结算各地区的永久每两周控制度；控制度不低于下限。
GER_region_game_daily_tick = {
    add_to_variable = { GER_region_day = 1 }
    set_temp_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = GER_region_day }
    modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 14 }
    # 威悉-埃姆斯
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_1 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_1 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_1 = 0 } } GER_region_action_1_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_2 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_2 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_2 = 0 } } GER_region_action_2_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_7 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_7 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_7 = 0 } } GER_region_action_7_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_3 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_3 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 168 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R01_act_3 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_3_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_3 = 0 } } GER_region_action_3_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_4 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_4 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 168 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R01_act_4 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_4_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_4 = 0 } } GER_region_action_4_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_5 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_5 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R01_act_5 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_5_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_5 = 0 } } GER_region_action_5_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_6 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_6 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R01_act_6 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_6_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_6 = 0 } } GER_region_action_6_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_20 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_20 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_act_20 = 0 } } GER_region_action_20_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy > 0 } }
        add_to_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy_days = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 1 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 9 } } GER_region_action_9_daily = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 10 } } GER_region_action_10_daily = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 11 } } GER_region_action_11_daily = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 12 } } GER_region_action_12_daily = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 13 } } GER_region_action_13_daily = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 14 } } GER_region_action_14_daily = yes }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R01_policy_days }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 9 } } GER_region_action_9_biweekly = yes }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 10 } } GER_region_action_10_biweekly = yes }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 11 } } GER_region_action_11_biweekly = yes }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 12 } } GER_region_action_12_biweekly = yes }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 13 } } GER_region_action_13_biweekly = yes }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_policy = 14 } } GER_region_action_14_biweekly = yes }
        }
    }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control_growth > 0 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = GER_region_R01_control_growth } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control < GER_region_R01_control_floor } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_control = GER_region_R01_control_floor } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_cd_6 > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_cd_6 = 1 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_leader = 1 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = 0.05 } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }   # 领导人 恩斯特·多伊米希
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_leader = 2 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = 0.05 } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }   # 领导人 罗伯特·迪斯曼
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_leader = 3 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = 0.05 } GER_region_R01_add_support = yes }   # 领导人 阿尔弗雷德·亨克
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_leader = 7 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = 0.1 } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }   # 领导人 保罗·迪特曼
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_leader = 8 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = 0.1 } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }   # 领导人 奥托·布劳恩
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_day_mod = 0 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_leader = 9 } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_delta = -0.2 } GER_region_R01_add_control = yes }   # 领导人 保罗·希尔施
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_return > 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_state = 2 } } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_return = 0 } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_days = 0 } }   # 临时摇摆区被别的效果改了类型：不再恢复
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_state = 3 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_state = 8 } } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_support < 50 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control < 60 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_return = GER_region_R01_state } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_state = 2 } }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_return > 0 } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_support < 50 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control < 60 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_days = 35 } }
        else = {
            subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_days = 1 }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_days < 1 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_state = GER_region_R01_swing_return } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_return = 0 } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_swing_days = 0 } }
        }
    }
    # 汉诺威
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_1 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_1 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_1 = 0 } } GER_region_action_1_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_2 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_2 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_2 = 0 } } GER_region_action_2_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_7 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_7 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_7 = 0 } } GER_region_action_7_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_3 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_3 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 168 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R02_act_3 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_3_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_3 = 0 } } GER_region_action_3_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_4 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_4 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 168 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R02_act_4 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_4_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_4 = 0 } } GER_region_action_4_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_5 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_5 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R02_act_5 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_5_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_5 = 0 } } GER_region_action_5_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_6 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_6 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = GER_region_R02_act_6 }
        modulo_temp_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 14 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_elapsed = 0 } } GER_region_action_6_biweekly = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_6 = 0 } } GER_region_action_6_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_20 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_20 = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_act_20 = 0 } } GER_region_action_20_complete = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_policy > 0 } }
        add_to_variable = { GER_region_R02_policy_days = 1 }
        set_variable = { GER_region_target = 2 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_policy = 9 } } GER_region_action_9_daily = yes }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R02_policy = 10 } } GER_region_action_10_daily = yes }

    GER_region_on_open_map = yes   # 手写钩子：各地区的开局数值设好之后（例如补上巴伐利亚危机结局对周边地区的影响）
    set_variable = { GER_region_selected = 0 }
    GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
}

# ---- 地区效益：按支持度 / 控制度算各地区档位（未开放 = 25 无效果），再给该地区里归德国所有的州加对应修正
GER_region_game_update_tiers = {
    # 威悉-埃姆斯
    if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_open = 1 } } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_tier = 25 } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control > 99.99 } check_variable = { GER_region_R01_support > 24.99 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_tier = 100 } }
    else = {
        set_temp_variable = { GER_region_half = GER_region_R01_support }
        multiply_temp_variable = { GER_region_half = 2 }
        subtract_from_temp_variable = { GER_region_half = GER_region_R01_control }   # 2 x 支持度 - 控制度 >= 0 即支持度够一半
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control > 74.99 } check_variable = { GER_region_half > -0.001 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_tier = 75 } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control > 49.99 } check_variable = { GER_region_half > -0.001 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_tier = 50 } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_R01_control > 24.99 } } set_variable = { GER_region_R01_tier = 25 } }
        else = { set_variable = { GER_region_R01_tier = 0 } }
    }
    56 = { set_temp_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = GER.GER_region_R01_tier } if = { limit = { NOT = { is_owned_by = GER } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = 25 } } GER_region_state_set_tier = yes set_temp_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = GER.GER_region_R01_leader } if = { limit = { OR = { NOT = { is_owned_by = GER } NOT = { check_variable = { GER.GER_region_R01_open = 1 } } } } set_temp_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 0 } } GER_region_state_set_leader = yes }

# 州作用域：只保留 GER_region_tier_now 对应的那个修正（25 = 全部去掉）
GER_region_state_clear_tiers = {
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_100 } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_100 } }
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_75 } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_75 } }
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_50 } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_50 } }
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_0 } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_0 } }
}
GER_region_state_set_tier = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = 100 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_100 } } } GER_region_state_clear_tiers = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_100 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = 75 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_75 } } } GER_region_state_clear_tiers = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_75 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = 50 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_50 } } } GER_region_state_clear_tiers = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_50 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = 0 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_0 } } } GER_region_state_clear_tiers = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_tier_0 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_tier_now = 25 } } GER_region_state_clear_tiers = yes }
}
# 州作用域：只保留 GER_region_leader_now（领导人序号）对应的领导人州修正
GER_region_state_clear_leader_mods = {
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_daeumig_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_daeumig_state } }
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_committee_nine_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_committee_nine_state } }
    if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_paul_dittmann_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_paul_dittmann_state } }
}
GER_region_state_set_leader = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 1 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_daeumig_state } } } GER_region_state_clear_leader_mods = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_daeumig_state } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 6 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_committee_nine_state } } } GER_region_state_clear_leader_mods = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_committee_nine_state } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 7 } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_paul_dittmann_state } } } GER_region_state_clear_leader_mods = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_region_leader_paul_dittmann_state } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 1 } check_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 6 } check_variable = { GER_region_leader_now = 7 } } } GER_region_state_clear_leader_mods = yes }
}

    clear_variable = GER_region_selected
    clr_country_flag = GER_region_map_opened
    clr_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase
    clr_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase
    clear_variable = GER_region_bavaria_stage
    clear_variable = GER_region_silesia_stage
    GER_region_actions_on_reset = yes   # 手写：决议自己的全国状态（例如罢工的建造减速）
    GER_region_game_hide_all_overlays = yes
}

# ==== 阶段博弈：巴伐利亚危机（只开放 巴伐利亚；右侧列表只显示该阶段的决议 15、16、17、18、19、20、21、22）
# 开始。设计上在地图全开（GER_region_game_open_map）之前用；进度从 0 开始，这时列表是空的
GER_region_game_bavaria_start = {
    set_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase
    set_variable = { GER_region_bavaria_stage = 0 }
    set_variable = { GER_region_R09_open = 1 } set_variable = { GER_region_R09_state = 10 } set_variable = { GER_region_R09_support = 25 } set_variable = { GER_region_R09_control = 25 }   # 魏玛联盟控制区
    set_variable = { GER_region_R09_leader = 13 }   # 约翰内斯·霍夫曼
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_15
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_16
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_17
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_18
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_19
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_20
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_act_20
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_21
    clear_variable = GER_region_R09_count_22
    GER_region_bavaria_on_start = yes   # 手写钩子
    set_variable = { GER_region_selected = 9 }
    GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
}
# 进度 +1（最大 4），右侧列表随之刷新
GER_region_game_bavaria_next_stage = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_bavaria_stage < 4 } } add_to_variable = { GER_region_bavaria_stage = 1 } }
    GER_region_game_sync_selected = yes
}
# 结束：该阶段的决议全部隐藏，巴伐利亚地块保留显示。阶段没在进行时什么都不做
GER_region_game_bavaria_end = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase }
        GER_region_bavaria_on_end = yes   # 手写钩子（例如去掉还在生效的修正）
        clr_country_flag = GER_region_bavaria_phase
        clear_variable = GER_region_R09_act_20
        GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
    }
}

# ==== 阶段博弈：西里西亚危机（只开放 西里西亚；右侧列表只显示该阶段的决议 23、24、25）
# 开始。设计上在地图全开（GER_region_game_open_map）之前用；进度从 0 开始，这时列表是空的
GER_region_game_silesia_start = {
    set_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase
    set_variable = { GER_region_silesia_stage = 0 }
    set_variable = { GER_region_R07_open = 1 } set_variable = { GER_region_R07_state = 2 } set_variable = { GER_region_R07_support = 15 } set_variable = { GER_region_R07_control = 5 }   # 摇摆区
    set_variable = { GER_region_R07_leader = 11 }   # 约瑟夫·比塔
    clear_variable = GER_region_R07_count_23
    clear_variable = GER_region_R07_count_24
    clear_variable = GER_region_R07_count_25
    GER_region_silesia_on_start = yes   # 手写钩子
    set_variable = { GER_region_selected = 7 }
    GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
}
# 进度 +1（最大 2），右侧列表随之刷新
GER_region_game_silesia_next_stage = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { GER_region_silesia_stage < 1 } } add_to_variable = { GER_region_silesia_stage = 1 } }
    GER_region_game_sync_selected = yes
}
# 结束：该阶段的决议全部隐藏，西里西亚地块保留显示。阶段没在进行时什么都不做
GER_region_game_silesia_end = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase }
        GER_region_silesia_on_end = yes   # 手写钩子（例如去掉还在生效的修正）
        clr_country_flag = GER_region_silesia_phase
        GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
    }
}

# 国防法危机：五条决议各 90 天，逐日递减，归零时记旗标（在 on_daily_GER 里调用）
GER_defense_law_tick = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_dl_settled } }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_draft > 0 } }
            subtract_from_variable = { GER_dl_days_draft = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_draft < 1 } }
                set_variable = { GER_dl_days_draft = 0 }
                set_country_flag = GER_dl_draft
                GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_suppress > 0 } }
            subtract_from_variable = { GER_dl_days_suppress = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_suppress < 1 } }
                set_variable = { GER_dl_days_suppress = 0 }
                set_country_flag = GER_dl_suppress
                GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_officers > 0 } }
            subtract_from_variable = { GER_dl_days_officers = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_officers < 1 } }
                set_variable = { GER_dl_days_officers = 0 }
                set_country_flag = GER_dl_workers_officers
                GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_amend > 0 } }
            subtract_from_variable = { GER_dl_days_amend = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_amend < 1 } }
                set_variable = { GER_dl_days_amend = 0 }
                set_country_flag = GER_dl_amend
                GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_neutral > 0 } }
            subtract_from_variable = { GER_dl_days_neutral = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { GER_dl_days_neutral < 1 } }
                set_variable = { GER_dl_days_neutral = 0 }
                set_country_flag = GER_dl_neutral
                GER_region_game_sync_frames = yes
            }
        }
    }
}
